b_hat[item_idx_np] - d_hat[judge_idx_np]\\n    pred = expit(logits)\\n    residuals = y_np - pred\\n\\n    r_ni = np.full((N, I), np.nan)\\n    r_ni_counts = np.zeros((N, I), dtype=int)\\n    for k in range(len(residuals)):\\n        n, i = model_idx_np[k], item_idx_np[k]\\n        if np.isnan(r_ni[n, i]):\\n            r_ni[n, i] = 0.0\\n        r_ni[n, i] += residuals[k]\\n        r_ni_counts[n, i] += 1\\n\\n    valid = r_ni_counts > 0\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"}

\\\"cuda,cpu\\\")\\nos.environ.setdefault(\\\"XLA_PYTHON_CLIENT_PREALLOCATE\\\", \\\"false\\\")\\nos.environ.setdefault(\\\"XLA_PYTHON_CLIENT_MEM_FRACTION\\\", \\\"0.25\\\")\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport sys\\nimport time\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\n\\nBASE_DIR = \\\"/root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias\\\"\\n\\nEXCLUDE_JUDGES = [\\\"mistral-large-2\\\"]\\nEXCLUDE_MODELS_FROM_TAU = [\\\"qwen3-8b\\\"]\\n\\n\\ndef filter_excluded_judges(data: dict, exclude: list[str]) -> dict:\\n    judge_names = data[\\\"judge_names\\\"]\\n    excluded_set = set(exclude) & set(judge_names)\\n    if not excluded_set:\\n        print(f\\\"  No judges to exclude (requested: {exclude})\\\")\\n        return data\\n\\n    excluded_indices = {judge_names.index(j) for j in excluded_set}\\n    mask = np.array([j not in excluded_indices for j in data[\\\"judge_idx\\\"]])\\n\\n    remaining = [j for j in judge_names if j not in excluded_set]\\n    old_to_new = {}\\n    for old_j, name in enumerate(judge_names):\\n        if name not in excluded_set:\\n            old_to_new[old_j] = len(old_to_new)\\n\\n    n_removed = (~mask).sum()\\n    print(f\\\"  Excluded judges {sorted(excluded_set)}: removed {n_removed} observations\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Remaining judges ({len(remaining)}): {remaining}\\\")\\n\\n    return {\\n        \\\"model_idx\\\": data[\\\"model_idx\\\"][mask],\\n        \\\"item_idx\\\": data[\\\"item_idx\\\"][mask],\\n        \\\"judge_idx\\\": np.array([old_to_new[j] for j in data[\\\"judge_idx\\\"][mask]], dtype=np.int32),\\n        \\\"y\\\": data[\\\"y\\\"][mask],\\n        \\\"model_names\\\": data[\\\"model_names\\\"],\\n        \\\"item_ids\\\": data[\\\"item_ids\\\"],\\n        \\\"judge_names\\\": remaining,\\n        \\\"e_i\\\": data[\\\"e_i\\\"],\\n        \\\"N\\\": data[\\\"N\\\"],\\n        \\\"I\\\": data[\\\"I\\\"],\\n        \\\"J\\\": len(remaining),\\n        \\\"n_obs\\\": int(mask.sum()),\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef filter_items_by_coverage(data: dict, min_judges: int) -> dict:\\n    item_idx = data[\\\"item_idx\\\"]\\n    judge_idx = data[\\\"judge_idx\\\"]\\n    I = data[\\\"I\\\"]\\n\\n    judges_per_item = [set() for _ in range(I)]\\n    for k in range(len(item_idx)):\\n        judges_per_item[item_idx[k]].add(judge_idx[k])\\n\\n    coverage = np.array([len(s) for s in judges_per_item])\\n    keep_items = set(np.where(coverage >= min_judges)[0])\\n    n_dropped = I - len(keep_items)\\n\\n    if n_dropped == 0:\\n        print(f\\\"  All {I} items have >= {min_judges} judge coverage\\\")\\n        return data\\n\\n    mask = np.array([item_idx[k] in keep_items for k in range(len(item_idx))])\\n    old_to_new = {}\\n    for old_i in sorted(keep_items):\\n        old_to_new[old_i] = len(old_to_new)\\n\\n    new_item_ids = [data[\\\"item_ids\\\"][old_i] for old_i in sorted(keep_items)]\\n    new_e_i = data[\\\"e_i\\\"][sorted(keep_items)]\\n    new_I = len(keep_items)\\n\\n    print(f\\\"  Items: {I} -> {new_I} (dropped {n_dropped} with <{min_judges} judges)\\\")\\n\\n    return {\\n        \\\"model_idx\\\": data[\\\"model_idx\\\"][mask],\\n        \\\"item_idx\\\": np.array([old_to_new[item_idx[k]] for k in range(len(item_idx)) if mask[k]], dtype=np.int32),\\n        \\\"judge_idx\\\": data[\\\"judge_idx\\\"][mask],\\n        \\\"y\\\": data[\\\"y\\\"][mask],\\n        \\\"model_names\\\": data[\\\"model_names\\\"],\\n        \\\"item_ids\\\": new_item_ids,\\n        \\\"judge_names\\\": data[\\\"judge_names\\\"],\\n        \\\"e_i\\\": new_e_i,\\n        \\\"N\\\": data[\\\"N\\\"],\\n        \\\"I\\\": new_I,\\n        \\\"J\\\": data[\\\"J\\\"],\\n        \\\"n_obs\\\": int(mask.sum()),\\n    }\\n\\n\\ndef convergence_stoploss(idata, output_dir):\\n    import arviz as az\\n\\n    rhat = az.rhat(idata)\\n    ess_bulk = az.ess(idata, method=\\\"bulk\\\")\\n\\n    rhat_max = max(\\n        float(rhat[v].values.max()) if rhat[v].values.ndim > 0 else float(rhat[v].values)\\n        for v in rhat.data_vars\\n    )\\n    ess_min = min(\\n        float(ess_bulk[v].values.min()) if ess_bulk[v].values.ndim > 0 else float(ess_bulk[v].values)\\n        for v in ess_bulk.data_vars\\n    )\\n\\n    print(f\\\"\\\\n=== CONVERGENCE CHECK ===\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"R-hat max: {rhat_max:.4f} (threshold: 1.1)\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"ESS min: {ess_min:.0f} (threshold: 50)\\\")\\n\\n    if rhat_max > 1.1 or ess_min < 50:\\n        print(\\\"STOP-LOSS TRIGGERED: R-hat > 1.1 or ESS < 50\\\")\\n        with open(os.path.join(output_dir, \\\"CONVERGENCE_FAILED\\\"), \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n            f.write(f\\\"rhat_max={rhat_max}, ess_min={ess_min}\\\\n\\\")\\n        return False\\n    else:\\n        print(\\\"Convergence OK\\\")\\n        with open(os.path.join(output_dir, \\\"CONVERGENCE_OK\\\"), \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n            f.write(f\\\"rhat_max={rhat_max}, ess_min={ess_min}\\\\n\\\")\\n        return True\\n\\n\\ndef save_method_results(name, res, output_dir):\\n    import arviz as az\\n\\n    np.save(os.path.join(output_dir, f\\\"{name}_theta.npy\\\"), res[\\\"theta_hat\\\"])\\n    if \\\"c_hat\\\" in res:\\n        np.save(os.path.join(output_dir, f\\\"{name}_c.npy\\\"), res[\\\"c_hat\\\"])\\n    if \\\"b_hat\\\" in res:\\n        np.save(os.path.join(output_dir, f\\\"{name}_b.npy\\\"), res[\\\"b_hat\\\"])\\n    if \\\"d_hat\\\" in res:\\n        np.save(os.path.join(output_dir, f\\\"{name}_d.npy\\\"), res[\\\"d_hat\\\"])\\n\\n    mcmc_obj = res.get(\\\"mcmc\\\")\\n    if mcmc_obj is not None:\\n        try:\\n            idata = az.from_numpyro(mcmc_obj)\\n            nc_path = os.path.join(output_dir, f\\\"{name}_idata.nc\\\")\\n            idata.to_netcdf(nc_path)\\n            print(f\\\"  Saved InferenceData: {nc_path}\\\")\\n        except Exception as e:\\n            print(f\\\"  Warning: failed to save InferenceData for {name}: {e}\\\")\\n\\n    sys.stdout.flush()\\n\\n\\n# ---- Non-centered models ----\\n\\ndef _mfrm_model_ncp(model_idx, item_idx, judge_idx, y, N, I, J):\\n    import jax.numpy as jnp\\n    import numpyro\\n    import numpyro.distributions as dist\\n\\n    sigma_theta = numpyro.sample(\\\"sigma_theta\\\", dist.HalfNormal(1.0))\\n    theta_raw = numpyro.sample(\\\"theta_raw\\\", dist.Normal(0, 1).expand([N]))\\n    theta_uncentered = theta_raw * sigma_theta\\n    theta = numpyro.deterministic(\\\"theta\\\", theta_uncentered - theta_uncentered.mean())\\n\\n    sigma_b = numpyro.sample(\\\"sigma_b\\\", dist.HalfNormal(1.0))\\n    b_raw = numpyro.sample(\\\"b_raw\\\", dist.Normal(0, 1).expand([I]))\\n    b = numpyro.deterministic(\\\"b\\\", b_raw * sigma_b)\\n\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"}

AB/BA position pairs are averaged; if average=0.5 (conflicting), treated as tie and discarded\\n  3. gpt-4-0314 self-comparison (model_a == baseline) is excluded\\n\\nDependencies: pandas, numpy, pathlib, json\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport json\\nfrom collections import defaultdict\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport numpy as np\\nimport pandas as pd\\n\\n\\nBASELINE_MODEL = \\\"gpt-4-0314\\\"\\n\\n\\ndef load_judgments(judgment_dir: str) -> list[dict]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Read all per-judge JSONL files from subdirectories.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    judgment_dir = Path(judgment_dir)\\n    records = []\\n    jsonl_files = sorted(judgment_dir.glob(\\\"*/arena_hard_judgments.jsonl\\\"))\\n    if not jsonl_files:\\n        jsonl_files = sorted(judgment_dir.glob(\\\"arena_hard_judgments.jsonl\\\"))\\n\\n    for fpath in jsonl_files:\\n        with open(fpath) as f:\\n            for line in f:\\n                line = line.strip()\\n                if not line:\\n                    continue\\n                rec = json.loads(line)\\n                if rec.get(\\\"status\\\") != \\\"ok\\\":\\n                    continue\\n                score = rec.get(\\\"score\\\")\\n                if score is None or score == \\\"\\\":\\n                    continue\\n                score = float(score)\\n                records.append({\\n                    \\\"item_id\\\": rec[\\\"item_id\\\"],\\n                    \\\"model\\\": rec[\\\"model_a\\\"],\\n                    \\\"judge\\\": rec[\\\"judge\\\"],\\n                    \\\"score\\\": score,\\n                    \\\"position\\\": rec[\\\"position\\\"],\\n                })\\n    return records\\n\\n\\ndef preprocess(records: list[dict]) -> list[dict]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Apply the three design decisions: exclude self-comparison, average positions, drop ties.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    # 1. Exclude baseline self-comparison\\n    records = [r for r in records if r[\\\"model\\\"] != BASELINE_MODEL]\\n\\n    # 2. Group by (model, item, judge) and average AB/BA positions\\n    grouped = defaultdict(list)\\n    for r in records:\\n        key = (r[\\\"model\\\"], r[\\\"item_id\\\"], r[\\\"judge\\\"])\\n        grouped[key].append(r[\\\"score\\\"])\\n\\n    observations = []\\n    n_ties_dropped = 0\\n    for (model, item_id, judge), scores in grouped.items():\\n        avg_score = np.mean(scores)\\n        # 3. Drop ties\\n        if abs(avg_score - 0.5) < 1e-9:\\n            n_ties_dropped += 1\\n            continue\\n        y = 1 if avg_score > 0.5 else 0\\n        observations.append({\\n            \\\"model\\\": model,\\n            \\\"item_id\\\": item_id,\\n            \\\"judge\\\": judge,\\n            \\\"y\\\": y,\\n        })\\n\\n    print(f\\\"  Ties dropped (including conflicting AB/BA): {n_ties_dropped}\\\")\\n    return observations\\n\\n\\ndef build_indices(observations: list[dict]) -> tuple[dict, dict, dict]:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Build name-to-index mappings, sorted alphabetically.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    models = sorted({o[\\\"model\\\"] for o in observations})\\n    items = sorted({o[\\\"item_id\\\"] for o in observations})\\n    judges = sorted({o[\\\"judge\\\"] for o in observations})\\n    model_to_idx = {m: i for i, m in enumerate(models)}\\n    item_to_idx = {it: i for i, it in enumerate(items)}\\n    judge_to_idx = {j: i for i, j in enumerate(judges)}\\n    return model_to_idx, item_to_idx, judge_to_idx\\n\\n\\ndef load_exposure(exposure_csv: str, item_to_idx: dict) -> np.ndarray:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Load exposure scores and align to item indices.\\n\\n    Items in judgments but not in exposure get e_i=0 (no exposure info).\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    df = pd.read_csv(exposure_csv)\\n    n_items = len(item_to_idx)\\n    e = np.zeros(n_items, dtype=np.float64)\\n    matched = 0\\n    for _, row in df.iterrows():\\n        iid = row[\\\"item_id\\\"]\\n        if iid in item_to_idx:\\n            e[item_to_idx[iid]] = float(row[\\\"e_i\\\"])\\n            matched += 1\\n    print(f\\\"  Exposure: {matched}/{n_items} items matched ({len(df)} in CSV)\\\")\\n    return e\\n\\n\\ndef load_data(judgment_dir: str, exposure_csv: str) -> dict:\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Full pipeline: load, preprocess, index, align exposure.\\n\\n    Returns dict with:\\n      - model_idx, item_idx, judge_idx, y: np arrays (sparse observations)\\n      - model_names, item_ids, judge_names: lists (index -> name)\\n      - e_i: exposure array aligned to item indices\\n      - N, I, J, n_obs: dimension counts\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    print(\\\"Loading judgments...\\\")\\n    records = load_judgments(judgment_dir)\\n    print(f\\\"  Raw records loaded: {len(records)}\\\")\\n\\n    print(\\\"Preprocessing...\\\")\\n    observations = preprocess(records)\\n    print(f\\\"  Binary observations after preprocessing: {len(observations)}\\\")\\n\\n    # Filter: keep only items judged by >= 3 judges\\n    print(\\\"Filtering items by minimum judge coverage...\\\")\\n    judges_per_item = defaultdict(set)\\n    for o in observations:\\n        judges_per_item[o[\\\"item_id\\\"]].add(o[\\\"judge\\\"])\\n    min_judges = 3\\n    valid_items = {item for item, judges in judges_per_item.items() if len(judges) >= min_judges}\\n    n_before = len(observations)\\n    items_before = len(judges_per_item)\\n    observations = [o for o in observations if o[\\\"item_id\\\"] in valid_items]\\n    items_after = len(valid_items)\\n    print(f\\\"  Items: {items_before} -> {items_after} (dropped {items_before - items_after} with <{min_judges} judges)\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Observations: {n_before} -> {len(observations)}\\\")\\n\\n    print(\\\"Building indices...\\\")\\n    model_to_idx, item_to_idx, judge_to_idx = build_indices(observations)\\n    N = len(model_to_idx)\\n    I = len(item_to_idx)\\n    J = len(judge_to_idx)\\n    print(f\\\"  Models (N): {N}, Items (I): {I}, Judges (J): {J}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Sparsity: {len(observations)}/{N*I*J} = {len(observations)/(N*I*J):.1%}\\\")\\n\\n    model_idx = np.array([model_to_idx[o[\\\"model\\\"]] for o in observations], dtype=np.int32)\\n    item_idx = np.array([item_to_idx[o[\\\"item_id\\\"]] for o in observations], dtype=np.int32)\\n    judge_idx = np.array([judge_to_idx[o[\\\"judge\\\"]] for o in observations], dtype=np.int32)\\n    y = np.array([o[\\\"y\\\"] for o in observations], dtype=np.float32)\\n\\n    print(\\\"Loading exposure scores...\\\")\\n    e_i = load_exposure(exposure_csv, item_to_idx)\\n\\n    model_names = sorted(model_to_idx, key=model_to_idx.get)\\n    item_ids = sorted(item_to_idx, key=item_to_idx.get)\\n    judge_names = sorted(judge_to_idx, key=judge_to_idx.get)\\n\\n    # Summary statistics\\n    print(\\\"\\\\n=== Data Summary ===\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Models: {model_names}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Judges: {judge_names}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Items: {I}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Observations: {len(y)}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Win rate (y=1): {y.mean():.3f}\\\")\\n    print(f\\\"  Exposure range: [{e_i.min():.3f}, {e_i.max():.3f}], mean={e_i.mean():.3f}\\\")\\n\\n    # Per-model win rates\\n    print(\\\"\\\\n  Per-model win rates:\\\")\\n    for n, name in enumerate(model_names):\\n        mask = model_idx == n\\n        print(f\\\"    {name}: {y[mask].mean():.3f} (n={mask.sum()})\\\")\\n\\n    # Per-judge observation counts\\n    print(\\\"\\\\n  Per-judge observations:\\\")\\n    for j, name in enumerate(judge_names):\\n        mask = judge_idx == j\\n        print(f\\\"    {name}: {mask.sum()}\\\")\\n\\n    return {\\n        \\\"model_idx\\\": model_idx,\\n        \\\"item_idx\\\": item_idx,\\n        \\\"judge_idx\\\": judge_idx,\\n        \\\"y\\\": y,\\n        \\\"model_names\\\": model_names,\\n        \\\"item_ids\\\": item_ids,\\n        \\\"judge_names\\\": judge_names,\\n        \\\"e_i\\\": e_i,\\n        \\\"N\\\": N,\\n        \\\"I\\\": I,\\n        \\\"J\\\": J,\\n        \\\"n_obs\\\": len(y),\\n    }\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    import sys\\n    jdir = sys.argv[1] if len(sys.argv) > 1 else \\\"/root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp001/results\\\"\\n    ecsv = sys.argv[2] if len(sys.argv) > 2 else \\\"/root/gated_mfrm_judge_bias/exp002/results/exposure_scores.csv\\\"\\n    data = load_data(jdir, ecsv)\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.44}}"}

target_accept_prob=target_accept)\n    mcmc = MCMC(kernel, num_warmup=warmup, num_samples=samples,\n                num_chains=chains, chain_method=\"vectorized\", progress_bar=True)\n\n    t0 = time.time()\n    mcmc.run(jax.random.PRNGKey(seed),\n             model_idx=model_idx, item_idx=item_idx, judge_idx=judge_idx,\n             y=y_jnp, e=e_jnp, N=data[\"N\"], I=data[\"I\"], J=data[\"J\"])\n    elapsed = time.time() - t0\n    print(f\"  MCMC done in {elapsed:.1f}s\")\n\n    samples_dict = mcmc.get_samples()\n    theta_hat = _to_numpy(samples_dict[\"theta\"].mean(axis=0))\n    b_hat = _to_numpy(samples_dict[\"b\"].mean(axis=0))\n    d_hat = _to_numpy(samples_dict[\"d\"].mean(axis=0))\n    c_hat = _to_numpy(samples_dict[\"c\"].mean(axis=0)) if \"c\" in samples_dict else None\n\n    # Convergence diagnostics\n    idata = az.from_numpyro(mcmc)\n    rhat = az.rhat(idata)\n    ess_bulk = az.ess(idata, method=\"bulk\")\n\n    rhat_max = max(\n        float(rhat[v].values.max()) if rhat[v].values.ndim > 0 else float(rhat[v].values)\n        for v in rhat.data_vars\n    )\n    ess_min = min(\n        float(ess_bulk[v].values.min()) if ess_bulk[v].values.ndim > 0 else float(ess_bulk[v].values)\n        for v in ess_bulk.data_vars\n    )\n\n    print(f\"\\n  Convergence: R-hat max={rhat_max:.4f}, ESS min={ess_min:.0f}\")\n    if rhat_max > 1.1:\n        print(f\"  WARNING: R-hat > 1.1 — convergence issue!\")\n\n    # Save\n    np.save(os.path.join(output_dir, \"theta.npy\"), theta_hat)\n    np.save(os.path.join(output_dir, \"b.npy\"), b_hat)\n    np.save(os.path.join(output_dir, \"d.npy\"), d_hat)\n    if c_hat is not None:\n        np.save(os.path.join(output_dir, \"c.npy\"), c_hat)\n    idata.to_netcdf(os.path.join(output_dir, \"idata.nc\"))\n\n    return {\n        \"theta_hat\": theta_hat, \"b_hat\": b_hat, \"d_hat\": d_hat, \"c_hat\": c_hat,\n        \"rhat_max\": rhat_max, \"ess_min\": ess_min, \"elapsed\": elapsed,\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_rankings(theta_hat, model_names, exclude_from_tau):\n    \"\"\"Compute Arena rank correlation (Kendall tau, Spearman rho).\"\"\"\n    matched = []\n    for i, m in enumerate(model_names):\n        if m in exclude_from_tau:\n            continue\n        elo_key = ARENA_NAME_MAPPING.get(m, m)\n        if elo_key in ARENA_ELO:\n            matched.append((i, m, ARENA_ELO[elo_key]))\n\n    if len(matched) < 3:\n        return None\n\n    idx, names, elos = zip(*matched)\n    thetas = [theta_hat[i] for i in idx]\n\n    tau, tau_p = kendalltau(elos, thetas)\n    rho, rho_p = spearmanr(elos, thetas)\n\n    # Compute ranks\n    elo_arr = np.array(elos)\n    theta_arr = np.array(thetas)\n    arena_ranks = len(elo_arr) - np.argsort(np.argsort(elo_arr))\n    theta_ranks = len(theta_arr) - np.argsort(np.argsort(theta_arr))\n\n    ranking_table = []\n    for k in range(len(names)):\n        ranking_table.append({\n            \"model\": names[k],\n            \"theta\": thetas[k],\n            \"arena_elo\": elos[k],\n            \"arena_rank\": int(arena_ranks[k]),\n            \"theta_rank\": int(theta_ranks[k]),\n        })\n    ranking_table.sort(key=lambda x: -x[\"theta\"])\n\n    return {\n        \"tau\": tau, \"tau_p\": tau_p,\n        \"rho\": rho, \"rho_p\": rho_p,\n        \"n_models\": len(matched),\n        \"ranking_table\": ranking_table,\n    }\n\n\ndef fit_raw_average(data):\n    N = data[\"N\"]\n    model_idx = data[\"model_idx\"]\n    y = data[\"y\"]\n    theta_hat = np.zeros(N)\n    for n in range(N):\n        mask = model_idx == n\n        if mask.sum() > 0:\n            theta_hat[n] = y[mask].mean()\n    theta_hat = theta_hat - theta_hat.mean()\n    return theta_hat\n\n\ndef format_results(name, result, rankings, model_names):\n    lines = []\n    lines.append(f\"\\n{'='*70}\")\n    lines.append(f\"  {name}\")\n    lines.append(f\"{'='*70}\")\n    lines.append(f\"  R-hat max: {result['rhat_max']:.4f}\")\n    lines.append(f\"  ESS min:   {result['ess_min']:.0f}\")\n    lines.append(f\"  Time:      {result['elapsed']:.1f}s\")\n\n    if result[\"c_hat\"] is not None:\n        c = result[\"c_hat\"]\n        lines.append(f\"  c_n range: [{c.min():.3f}, {c.max():.3f}], mean={c.mean():.3f}\")\n\n    if rankings:\n        lines.append(f\"\\n  Kendall tau = {rankings['tau']:.3f} (p={rankings['tau_p']:.4f})\")\n        lines.append(f\"  Spearman rho = {rankings['rho']:.3f} ELO':>10} {'A.Rank':>7} {'T.Rank':>7}\")\n        lines.append(f\"  {'-'*62}\")\n        for r in rankings[\"ranking_table\"]:\n            lines.append(f\"  {r['model']:<30} {r['theta']:>8.3f} print(\"=\" * 60)\n    data = load_data(judgment_dir, exposure_csv)\n\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"STEP 2: Filtering\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    data = filter_excluded_judges(data, EXCLUDE_JUDGES)\n    data = filter_items_by_coverage(data, min_judges=3)\n    print(f\"  Final: N={data['N']}, I={data['I']}, J={data['J']}, n_obs={data['n_obs']}\")\n\n    # Raw average baseline\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\" * 60)\n    print(\"STEP 3: Raw average baseline\")\n    print(\"=\" * 60)\n    raw_theta = fit_raw_average(data)\n    raw_rankings = compute_rankings(raw_theta, data[\"model_names\"], EXCLUDE_MODELS_FROM_TAU)\n    print(f\"  Raw average: tau={raw_rankings['tau']:.3f}, rho={raw_rankings['rho']:.3f}\")\n\n    # Variant A: c_n = 0\n    result_a = run_variant(\n        \"Variant A: c_n=0 (pure MFRM/NCP)\", _model_variant_a, data,\n        os.path.join(base_output, \"variant_a\"),\n        warmup=2000, samples=2000, chains=4, seed=42,\n    )\n    rankings_a = compute_rankings(result_a[\"theta_hat\"], data[\"model_names\"], EXCLUDE_MODELS_FROM_TAU)\n\n    # Clear GPU memory before Variant B\n    jax.clear_caches()\n\n    # Variant B: Tighter prior\n    result_b = run_variant(\n        \"Variant B: tighter c_n prior (sigma_c~HN(0.3))\", _model_variant_b, data,\n        os.path.join(base_output, \"variant_b\"),\n        warmup=2000, samples=2000, chains=4, seed=42,\n    )\n    rankings_b = compute_rankings(result_b[\"theta_hat\"], data[\"model_names\"], EXCLUDE_MODELS_FROM_TAU)\n\n    # Summary report\n    report_lines = []\n    report_lines.append(\"=\" * 70)\n    report_lines.append(\"  exp-020 Diagnosis: Oracle MCMC Variants\")\n    report_lines.append(\"  \" + time.strftime(\"%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S\"))\n    report_lines.append(\"=\" * 70)\n    report_lines.append(f\"\\nData: N={data['N']}, I={data['I']}, J={data['J']}, n_obs={data['n_obs']}\")\n    report_lines.append(f\"Excluded judges: {EXCLUDE_JUDGES}\")\n    report_lines.append(f\"Excluded from tau: {EXCLUDE_MODELS_FROM_TAU}\")\n    report_lines.append(f\"MCMC config: warmup=2000, samples=2000, chains=4, target_accept=0.95\")\n\n    # Raw average\n    report_lines.append(f\"\\n{'='*70}\")\n    report_lines.append(f\"  Baseline: Raw Average\")\n    report_lines.append(f\"{'='*70}\")\n    report_lines.append(f\"  Kendall tau = {raw_rankings['tau']:.3f} (p={raw_rankings['tau_p']:.4f})\")\n    report_lines.append(f\"  Spearman rho = {raw_rankings['rho']:.3f} (p={raw_rankings['rho_p']:.4f})\")\n\n    report_lines.append(format_results(\"Variant A: c_n=0 (pure MFRM/NCP)\", result_a, rankings_a, data[\"model_names\"]))\n    report_lines.append(format_results(\"Variant B: tighter c_n prior (sigma_c~HN(0.3))\", result_b, rankings_b, data[\"model_names\"]))\n\n    # Comparison table\n    report_lines.append(f\"\\n{'='*70}\")\n    report_lines.append(f\"  COMPARISON SUMMARY\")\n {rankings_b['rho']:>7.3f} {result_b['rhat_max']:>7.4f} {result_b['ess_min']:>7.0f}\")\n    report_lines.append(f\"  {'exp-020 v2 joint_gmfrm (reference)':<45} {'0.794':>7} {'---':>7} {'---':>7} tau_b = rankings_b[\"tau\"]\n    tau_raw = raw_rankings[\"tau\"]\n    if tau_a > 0.794:\n        report_lines.append(f\"  Variant A (no c_n) tau={tau_a:.3f} > 0.794 (G-MFRM v2)\")\n        report_lines.append(f\"  => Contamination gating IS hurting ranking accuracy.\")\n    else:\n        report_lines.append(f\"  Variant A (no c_n) tau={tau_a:.3f} <= 0.794 (G-MFRM v2)\")\n        report_lines.append(f\"  => Problem is NOT purely from contamination gating.\")\n\n    if tau_b > 0.794:\n        report_lines.append(f\"  Variant B (tighter prior) tau={tau_b:.3f} > 0.794 (G-MFRM (G-MFRM v2)\")\n        report_lines.append(f\"  => Tighter prior alone is not sufficient.\")\n\n    report = \"\\n\".join(report_lines) + \"\\n\"\n    print(report)\n\n    result_path =
